Across mathematical reasoning, knowledge QA, and code generation benchmarks on the Qwen family, ReLAT consistently improves over single-model inference, text-based collaboration, open-loop latent collaboration, and alternative test-time training objectives. On Qwen3-8B, ReLAT raises AIME 2024 accuracy from 56.7% to 73.3%, a 16.6-point gain over the strongest open-loop latent baseline.

</details>


### [191] [RedKnot: Efficient Long-Context LLM Serving with Head-Aware KV Reuse and SegPagedAttention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06256)
*Yang Liu,ZhaoKai Luo,HuaYi Jin,ZhiYong Wang,RuoZhou He,BoYu Wang,Guanjie Chen,Junhao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RedKnot提出了一种基于注意力头感知的KV缓存管理系统，将KV缓存从单一的Tensor抽象分解为头级别的结构化内存对象，从而在保持输出保真度的同时，高效支持位置无关的KV复用、前缀压缩、冷热分离和分布式部署，无需模型重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统将KV缓存视为同质的序列级内存块，忽略了不同注意力头在功能角色、注意力距离和运行时重要性上的结构化差异，导致资源利用率低下。

Method: RedKnot沿KV头维度分解缓存，识别每个头在不同服务场景下的重要性和有效注意力范围，将KV缓存从单一Tensor抽象转变为结构化内存对象，并统一支持多种缓存优化策略。

Result: 在不需模型重训练或微调的条件下，统一支持位置无关KV复用、前缀KV压缩、冷热KV分离和分布式KV放置，同时保持输出保真度并提升资源效率。

Conclusion: RedKnot将KV缓存从被动的运行时产物转变为动态的、模型感知的运行时基板，为可扩展的LLM服务建立了新的AI基础设施基础。

Abstract: As the input length of large language model (LLM) serving continues to grow, the KV cache has become a dominant bottleneck in AI infrastructure. It limits GPU memory capacity, serving concurrency, cache reuse, and distributed scalability. Several important problems, including position-independent KV cache, prefix KV cache compression, hot/cold KV cache separation, and distributed KV cache management, all depend on how the KV cache is represented and managed. However, existing serving systems largely rely on a monolithic KV cache abstraction, where the KV cache is treated as a homogeneous sequence of token-level memory blocks and managed with similar policies across attention heads and serving scenarios. We observe that KV cache utility is highly structured across KV heads: different heads exhibit different functional roles, attention distances, and runtime importance. Therefore, a full KV cache is not always necessary for every head, token range, or serving scenario.
  We present RedKnot, a head-aware KV cache management system for LLM serving. RedKnot breaks the conventional monolithic KV cache abstraction by decomposing the KV cache along KV heads, whose importance and effective attention ranges vary significantly across serving scenarios. This head-level decomposition turns the KV cache from a monolithic tensor abstraction into a structured memory object, enabling RedKnot to uniformly support position-independent KV reuse, prefix KV compression, hot/cold KV separation, and distributed KV placement while preserving output fidelity and improving resource efficiency, without requiring model retraining or fine-tuning. RedKnot establishes a new foundation for AI infrastructure by transforming the KV cache from a monolithic, passive runtime artifact into a dynamic, model-aware runtime substrate for scalable LLM serving.

</details>


### [192] [ToolChoiceConfusion: Causal Minimal Tool Filtering for Reliable LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06284)
*Rahul Suresh Babu,Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出因果最小工具过滤（CMTF）方法，通过因果充分性而非语义相关性选择工具，将每步暴露工具数从100降至1，减少约90%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具选择方法仅优化语义相关性，但相关工具可能不必要或时机不当，导致错误调用、过早操作和token浪费。

Method: 提出CMTF，利用轻量级前提-效果契约，基于因果充分性选择仅需的最小子集工具，无需训练。

Result: 在102个任务、100个工具、4种LLM后端、2448次运行的基准测试中，CMTF与最强因果基线成功率相当，但每步暴露工具从100减少到1，token使用减少约90%。

Conclusion: 因果充分性比语义相关性更适合工具选择，CMTF在不牺牲任务成功率的前提下大幅降低计算开销和错误调用。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on external tools, but larger tool menus can reduce reliability and efficiency by increasing wrong-tool calls, premature actions, and token cost. Existing tool-selection methods often optimize semantic relevance, exposing tools whose names or descriptions match the user request. We argue that relevance is insufficient: a tool may be related to the task while still being unnecessary or premature at the current step.
  We propose Causal Minimal Tool Filtering (CMTF), a training-free method that selects tools by causal sufficiency. CMTF uses lightweight precondition-effect contracts to expose only the minimal next-step tool frontier needed to advance from the current state toward the user goal. Across multi-step tool-use tasks, we compare CMTF with all-tools exposure, keyword retrieval, state-aware filtering, and causal-path ablations, measuring task success, wrong-tool calls, premature actions, tool exposure, and token cost. In the main benchmark with 102 tasks, 100 tools, four LLM backends, and 2448 task-method-model runs, CMTF matches the strongest causal baseline in aggregate success while reducing visible tools from 100 to one per step and reducing token usage by about 90% relative to all-tools exposure.

</details>


### [193] [TRACE: A Temporal Conditional Estimation for Multimodal Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06285)
*Ziwen Kan,Yishuo Chen,Kecheng Li,Andrew Wen,Xiaomeng Wang,Liwei Wang,Jihao Duan,Song Wang,Hongfang Liu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRACE，一种基于条件估计的多模态时间序列基础模型框架，解决时间错位和模态缺失问题，在医疗和情感计算基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际多模态场景中，时间序列常存在时间错位和部分模态缺失，现有方法依赖简单插补或掩码策略，未能利用跨模态依赖关系，导致表征质量下降。

Method: 提出TRACE条件估计范式，在缺失和不规则采样条件下，利用可用辅助模态系统性地推断不完整目标模态，从而增强多模态时间序列基础模型管道的鲁棒性。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV、CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI等基准上，TRACE在多种下游预测任务和缺失模态设置中持续优于先前多模态融合方法，对严重模态缺失表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TRACE有效解决了多模态时间序列中的时间错位和模态缺失问题，能够学习更可靠的跨模态表征，为实际多模态应用提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TS-FMs) aim to learn generalizable temporal representations that can be adapted to a wide range of downstream tasks. In real-world multimodal settings, time series are frequently affected by temporal misalignment and partial modality missingness, where different modalities are observed at heterogeneous time scales or are partially absent. Existing approaches typically rely on naive imputation or masking strategies, which fail to account for cross-modal dependencies and often lead to misaligned or degraded representations. We propose TRACE, a conditional estimation paradigm for multimodal time series foundation model pipelines under missingness and irregular sampling, allowing incomplete target modalities to be systematically inferred from available auxiliary modalities. We evaluate TRACE on diverse multimodal benchmarks spanning healthcare and affective computing, including the MIMIC-IV clinical dataset and the CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI benchmarks for multimodal sentiment analysis. Across a range of downstream prediction tasks and missing-modality settings, TRACE consistently outperforms prior multimodal fusion approaches, demonstrating improved robustness to severe modality missingness and more reliable cross-modal representations.

</details>


### [194] [Multi-ResNets for Subspace Preconditioning in Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06300)
*Merve Karakas,Christopher J. Williams,Emmanuel O. Balogun,Sadegh Sadeghi Tabas,Christian Brown,Nikhil Rao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MResOpt，一种分阶段残差神经网络架构，用于约束优化问题。它通过优先级分解约束满足、中间重新补全和阶段感知损失，在预测-补全-校正流水线中实现领域知识引导的有序约束满足。理论分析表明其无限宽极限行为等价于序列高斯过程回归。在QP、QCQP、SOCP和AC最优潮流任务上，该方法在高优先级约束满足和计算效率上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的约束优化方法难以有效优先级化约束满足，缺乏对约束间序结构的利用，且在学习过程中无法保证高优先级约束优先得到满足。

Method: 提出MResOpt分阶段残差神经网络，在预测-补全-校正流水线中，通过中间重新补全和阶段感知损失，按优先级分解约束满足过程；理论证明无限宽极限下等价于序列高斯过程回归。

Result: 在合成QP、QCQP、SOCP基准测试中，分阶段架构在凸和非凸场景下均改善了高优先级约束满足；在AC最优潮流任务上，基于物理启发的约束排序使MResOpt实现了比重新投影基线更低的高优先级违反，同时保持计算效率。

Conclusion: MResOpt通过分阶段残差架构有效实现了领域知识引导的有序约束满足，在多个约束优化问题上取得了更优的高优先级约束性能与计算效率的平衡。

Abstract: We propose MResOpt, a staged residual neural network architecture for constrained optimization problems. Our architecture fits within predict-complete-correct pipelines and decomposes constraint satisfaction by priority through intermediate re-completion and stage-aware losses. The framework enables domain-informed ordered constraint satisfaction which allows the network to utilize ordinal structure when present. Under an idealized infinite-width regime, we show that our design behaves as sequential Gaussian Process regression. On synthetic QP, QCQP, and SOCP benchmarks, the staged architecture improves high-priority constraint satisfaction across convex and non-convex settings. On line-flow-constrained AC optimal power flow, we introduce a physics-motivated constraint ordering and show that MResOpt supports a learned division of labor that keeps iterates on the equality manifold, achieving substantially lower high-priority violation than reprojected baselines while remaining computationally efficient.

</details>


### [195] [AIS-Based Vessel Trajectory Prediction Using Memory-Augmented Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06311)
*Wonmo Koo,Sanha Chang,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次实证研究了基于外部记忆的船舶轨迹预测方法，利用AIS数据在墨西哥湾和纽约湾的实验中，相较于未使用外部记忆的深度学习基线模型，取得了持续且显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管记忆增强神经网络在行人和车辆轨迹预测中表现优异，但其在船舶轨迹预测领域的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 基于自动识别系统（AIS）数据，采用外部记忆增强的神经网络进行船舶轨迹预测，并在墨西哥湾和纽约湾两个区域进行实验验证。

Result: 在墨西哥湾和纽约湾的数据实验中，该方法相较于未使用外部记忆的多种深度学习基线模型，均取得了持续且显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 记忆增强方法能有效提升船舶轨迹预测的准确性，对于海上避碰和航线优化具有重要价值。

Abstract: Accurate vessel trajectory prediction is essential for safe and efficient maritime operations, enabling collision avoidance and supporting route optimization. Although memory-augmented neural networks have recently shown strong performance in pedestrian and road-vehicle trajectory prediction by selectively retrieving relevant information from an external memory, their potential for vessel trajectory prediction remains underexplored. This paper presents an empirical investigation of memory-based trajectory prediction using Automatic Identification System (AIS) data. Experiments on data from the Gulf of Mexico and the New York Bight demonstrate consistent and substantial performance gains over a range of deep learning baselines that do not incorporate an external memory.

</details>


### [196] [LLM Self-Recognition: Steering and Retrieving Activation Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06315)
*Thibaud Ardoin,Jonas Schäfer,Gerhard Wunder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用随机稀疏向量扰动LLM内部残差流的方法，生成可检测的"指纹"，从而实现98%以上准确率的模型输出归属识别，且不影响生成文本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成内容日益泛滥，现有外部检测器效果有限；而LLM在生成文本中隐含自识别信号，但该能力的可靠性和可操控性尚未被充分探索。本文旨在利用模型内在表征结构实现高效归属检测。

Method: 在LLM生成过程中，对内部残差流施加随机稀疏向量进行扰动，形成独特的可检测指纹；随后利用另一个作为检测器的LLM的激活状态来恢复该信号，从而实现文本到特定模型的归属识别。

Result: 在多种检测设置下实现超过98%的归属准确率，同时生成文本质量未出现下降。

Conclusion: LLM具备可靠的自识别能力，且激活空间中存在可利用的结构，能够在不引起语义干扰的前提下编码归属信号。该方法为AI生成内容归属提供了一种实用且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in interpretability suggest that large language models (LLMs) implicitly encode signals in their generated text that enable self-recognition of their outputs. We demonstrate that this capability is reliable, even in low-entropy scenarios, and that it can be amplified through targeted intervention. By steering the internal residual stream during generation with a random sparse vector, we create a detectable fingerprint that enables attribution of a given text to a specific LLM. This signal is recoverable from the activations of an LLM used as a detector, achieving over 98% accuracy across multiple detection settings while preserving the quality of generated text. As AI-generated content proliferates, this approach offers a practical alternative to traditional detectors by leveraging the model's natural representation structure for attribution rather than embedding a signal externally. Our contributions include: (i) establishing reliable self-recognition capabilities in LLMs, (ii) a simple steering mechanism enabling multi-LLM identification with no quality degradation, (iii) demonstrating that activation spaces contain exploitable structure for encoding signals without semantic interference.

</details>


### [197] [DragOn: A Benchmark and Dataset for Drag-Based GUI Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06322)
*Nathan Bout,Maxime Langevin,Ronan Riochet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍DragOn，一个针对GUI代理中拖拽动作（如拖放、滑动、高亮）的基准测试和训练数据集，包含28.6万张截图和350万个训练任务，覆盖文本高亮、单元格选择、元素缩放和滑块操作四个领域。实验表明，在该数据集上微调可提升现有模型在计算机使用任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI代理在点击定位方面取得了显著进展，但拖拽动作（如拖放、滑动、高亮）的训练数据量级小了一个数量级，导致模型在复杂拖拽交互任务上表现不佳。

Method: 构建DragOn数据集，包含28.6万张训练截图和350万个训练任务，覆盖文本高亮、单元格选择、元素缩放和滑块操作四个领域，并提供一个2000样本的评估套件。在Qwen VLM模型上进行微调，并与GPT、Claude、Qwen、Kimi、Holo等模型进行比较评估。

Result: 该数据集能够提升现有最先进模型在下游计算机使用任务中的性能表现。

Conclusion: DragOn数据集有效弥补了GUI代理在拖拽动作方面的数据缺口，通过在该数据集上微调，可以显著提升模型处理复杂拖拽交互的能力。

Abstract: GUI agents - vision-based models that control desktops, web browsers, and mobile devices through graphical user interfaces - promise to automate a wide range of digital tasks. While million-scale datasets have enabled substantial progress on click-grounding, drag grounding (e.g. drag-and-drop, swipe, highlight) data remains an order of magnitude smaller and current models fall short on complex drag-based interactions. We introduce DragOn, a drag grounding benchmark and training dataset covering four domains: text highlighting, cell selection, element resizing and slider manipulation. The dataset comprises 286K training screenshots and 3.5M training tasks, plus a 2000-example held-out evaluation suite. We evaluate proprietary (GPT, Claude) and open-weight (Qwen, Kimi, Holo) models, as well as a Qwen VLM fine-tuned on our training data. Results suggest that our dataset could improve performance of state-of-the-art models on downstream computer-use tasks.

</details>


### [198] [TokenMizer: Graph-Structured Session Memory for Long-Horizon LLM Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06337)
*Shweta Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TokenMizer是一个开源代理系统，通过将LLM会话历史建模为类型化知识图谱，在显著降低token开销的同时，提高了长周期任务的结构化信息召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM部署面临上下文窗口有限的问题，当历史超出最大有效上下文窗口时，关键结构信息（如架构决策、任务转换、文件历史）会被丢弃。现有缓解方法将历史视为纯文本，破坏了会话可恢复所需的关系结构。

Method: TokenMizer将会话历史建模为类型化知识图谱（14种节点类型、7种边类型），采用混合提取流水线增量式填充图谱，三阶段检查点系统将图谱序列化为紧凑的恢复块，8层压缩流水线降低上下文开销，并配有语义缓存减少重复查询延迟。

Result: 在5个领域的21个会话基准测试中，恢复块平均78个token（范围42-124），比基线（159-170）小2倍；决策召回率提高9-17个百分点；平均任务召回51.0%、决策召回46.6%、文件召回58.7%；启发式压缩实现47.3%的token缩减。

Conclusion: TokenMizer提供了一种可查询的替代方案，以一半的token成本实现了优于文本保留基线的结构化信息保存能力，模糊标签匹配是改进任务召回率的主导因素。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) deployments for long-horizon tasks face a fundamental constraint: context windows are finite while productive work sessions are not. When history exceeds the Maximum Effective Context Window (MECW), critical structured information - architectural decisions, task transitions, file histories - is silently discarded. Existing mitigations treat history as flat text, destroying the relational structure that makes sessions resumable. We present TokenMizer, an open-source proxy system that models LLM session history as a typed knowledge graph. The schema defines 14 node types and 7 edge types. A hybrid extraction pipeline populates the graph incrementally, while a three-tier checkpoint system serializes it into compact resume blocks. An 8-layer compression pipeline reduces context overhead, and a semantic cache reduces repeated-query latency. Evaluated on a controlled benchmark of 21 sessions spanning 5 domains, TokenMizer demonstrates significant token economy. It produces resume blocks averaging 78 tokens (range: 42-124) - 2x smaller than evaluated baselines (159-170 tokens) - while achieving higher decision recall (+9-17 percentage points). Crucially, baselines only preserve that a technology was mentioned; TokenMizer preserves the rationale. Across all sessions, TokenMizer achieves mean task recall 51.0%, decision recall 46.6%, and file recall 58.7%. Variance reflects domain heterogeneity: explicit imperative phrasing (software engineering) scores higher than implicit reasoning (research). Ablation studies show fuzzy label matching is the dominant improvement factor (+33 pp task recall). The heuristic compression achieves 47.3% token reduction with zero external dependencies. TokenMizer provides a queryable alternative to text-retention baselines at half the token cost.

</details>


### [199] [Boosting Brain-to-Image Decoding with TRIBE v2 Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06345)
*Yohann Benchetrit,Marlène Careil,Simon Dahan,Hubert Banville,Stéphane d'Ascoli,Jean-Rémi King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何利用大规模fMRI编码模型TRIBE v2生成的合成数据来增强小样本真实fMRI数据集，从而提高大脑图像解码的性能，实验显示在Top-10图像检索准确率上最高可提升68%，甚至实现了零样本解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑解码受限于标记神经数据的稀缺性，在低数据量场景下表现不佳。为了解决这一问题，研究者探索是否可以通过预训练fMRI响应模型生成的合成数据来增强小样本真实数据集。

Method: 使用在大规模fMRI数据（超过1000小时视频、音频和语言刺激响应）上预训练的TRIBE v2编码模型生成合成fMRI数据，系统地评估了不同合成数据量对图像解码器性能的影响，并在7T fMRI Natural Scenes Dataset和3T fMRI BOLD5000两个数据集上进行了验证。

Result: 相比仅使用真实数据训练的模型，加入合成数据后Top-10图像检索准确率最高提升68%；合成数据与真实数据的比例需根据数据源调整；仅用合成fMRI数据训练的图像解码器在某些设置下仍能高于随机水平，实现了零样本大脑到图像解码。

Conclusion: 大规模fMRI响应模型（如TRIBE v2）可以作为基础，通过生成合成数据来显著提升图像解码的数据效率，为低数据场景下的脑解码提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Brain decoding is limited by the availability of labeled neural data, and remains challenging in low-data regimes. To address this issue, we investigate whether and when brain decoding can be boosted by augmenting small fMRI datasets with synthetic data generated by a pretrained model of fMRI responses to stimuli. We use TRIBE v2, a large encoding model pretrained on more than 1000 hours of fMRI responses to video, audio and language. For each dataset, we evaluate systematic grids that show how the performance of image decoders varies with the amount of synthetic data used for training. Our results, based on two datasets (the 7T fMRI Natural Scenes Dataset and 3T fMRI BOLD5000), show up to 68% improvement in Top-10 image-retrieval accuracy compared to decoders trained only on real data. Importantly, the proportion of augmented data required to reach a given image decoding performance needs to be adjusted depending on the data source. Surprisingly, image decoders trained exclusively on synthetic fMRI can perform above chance in some settings, suggesting that TRIBE v2 can support zero-shot brain-to-image decoding. Together, these results show how large-scale models of the fMRI responses to sight, sound and language may provide a foundation to improve the data efficiency for image decoding.

</details>


### [200] [Where Should Knowledge Enter? A Layered Framework for Knowledge Infusion in Multimodal Iterative Generative Mo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06356)
*Renjith Prasad,Chathurangi Shyalika,Anushka Pawar,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个四层知识注入框架（表面、轨迹、潜变量、参数），用于迭代式生成模型中的知识约束。在扩散模型的安全对齐实验中，累积使用表面+轨迹-潜变量三层注入，使知识违规输出减少70.97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识注入方法虽多，但通常按技术分类，缺乏从生成过程内部状态干预角度的统一视角。本文旨在提出一个结构化框架，将知识注入明确定位为对生成轨迹中不同组件的干预问题。

Method: 提出四层干预框架：表面层（输入/输出边界）、轨迹层（转移函数）、潜变量层（中间状态）、参数层（模型参数）。在扩散模型上实例化，并在安全对齐任务中使用多模态知识图谱，累积实现表面层（输入侧+输出侧）和轨迹-潜变量层（生成中期）三层注入。

Result: 每增加一层都能解决前一层无法覆盖的失败类型。相比原始生成，三层累积注入将知识违规输出减少70.97%，实验验证了框架的互补性预测。

Conclusion: 知识注入本质上是一个干预层问题，不同干预层具有互补性，多层组合能显著提升模型对结构化知识的遵从性，在安全关键场景中尤其有效。

Abstract: Multimodal generative models produce fluent outputs but remain unreliable when generation must respect structured, domain-specific, or safety-critical knowledge. Existing methods incorporate knowledge through mechanisms such as prompt augmentation, guidance, latent editing, or fine-tuning, yet they are typically categorized by technique rather than by the component of the generative process they modify. We argue that knowledge infusion in iterative generative models is fundamentally anintervention-layer problem. Since thegenerative process unfolds as a trajectory of internal states, knowledge can act on four structurally distinct components of this process: the input/output boundary, the transition function, the intermediate state, and the model parameters. This maps to four intervention layers: surface, trajectory, latent, and parametric infusion. We instantiate the framework in diffusion models, map representative methods to all four layers, and derive design principles for multi-layer composition. In a controlled safety-alignment experiment using a multimodal knowledge graph with two diffusion backbones, we implement three of the four layers cumulatively, surface (input-side and output-side) and trajectory--latent (mid-generation). We show empirically that each additional layer addresses failure classes that prior layers cannot reach, reducing knowledge-violating outputs by 70.97% compared to vanilla generation and empirically confirming the framework's complementarity prediction.

</details>


### [201] [An Infectious Disease Spread Simulation Based on Large Language Model Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06360)
*Yonchanok Khaokaew,Ruochen Kong,Andreas Zufle,Hao Xue,Taylor Anderson,Chandini Raina MacIntyre,Matthew Scotch,Flora D. Salim,David J Heslop*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究构建了基于空间信息和大语言模型的智能体仿真框架，模拟疫情中个人自报流感样疾病行为，揭示收入和受教育程度是报告率变化的主要驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传染病暴发期间个体决策行为对疫情动态和干预措施效果至关重要，现有研究虽已利用大语言模型模拟人类行为，但缺乏空间明确性和地理异质性的建模能力。

Method: 将LLM生成的决策集成到基于人口普查的合成人群智能体仿真框架中，以空间位置为核心特征，分配智能体至城市内空间单元，并在旧金山和亚特兰大比较三种决策场景：独立推理、家庭影响、信息框架效应。

Result: 收入和受教育程度是报告率变化的主导因素，地理因素、LLM模型选择和信息框架效应有较小但一致的影响。

Conclusion: 该框架可生成同时捕捉社会和地理异质性的合成数据，支持空间流行病学建模和有偏意识的行为分析。

Abstract: Modelling individual decision-making during infectious disease outbreaks is crucial for understanding behavioural dynamics and informing effective public health interventions. Prior work has shown that large language models can simulate realistic human behaviour by generating agent decisions based on demographic prompts and situational context. We build on this foundation with a spatially grounded, agent-based simulation framework that integrates LLM-generated decisions about self-reported influenza-like illness into a census-based synthetic population of agents. Location is treated as a central feature: agents are assigned to spatial units within cities, capturing the spatial distributions of different demographic groups using real-world census data and enabling geographically diverse behavioural modelling. We implement and compare three decision scenarios, independent reasoning, household influence, and message framing, and simulate self-reporting outcomes in San Francisco and Atlanta. Results reveal that income and education are the dominant drivers of reporting rate variation, with smaller but consistent effects from geography, LLM model choice, and message framing. Our framework generates synthetic data that captures both social and geographic heterogeneity, supporting spatial epidemiological modelling and bias-aware behavioural analysis.

</details>


### [202] [Rethinking Infrastructure Inspection as Image Difference Classification: A Traffic Sign Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06375)
*Ching Yau Fergus Mok,Lavindra de Silva,Varun Kumar Reja,Ioannis Brilakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将图像缺陷检测重新定义为图像差异分类（IDC），以减少对标注数据的依赖，并在低资源交通标志检测案例中验证了基于指令的分类器优于编码器方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生技术用于道路基础设施检测时受限于有限的标注数据，因此需要一种能减少数据依赖的方法。

Method: 利用资产连续状态监测的关系特性，将基于图像的缺陷检测重新表述为图像差异分类（IDC），并设计了基于指令的分类器与编码器分类器进行对比实验。

Result: 基于指令的分类器性能优于基于编码器的分类器，且通过与参考图像进行比较获得了更好的效果。

Conclusion: 图像差异分类（IDC）是一种有效的任务建模方式，能够应对基础设施检测中的数据约束问题，并可用于数字孪生资产状态的更新。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) allow the digitalization of road infrastructure inspection, though this is hindered by limited annotated data. This work exploits the relational nature of continuous asset condition monitoring to reformulate image-based defect detection as image difference classification (IDC) to reduce data reliance. This was evaluated in a case study on low-resource traffic sign inspection with different IDC classifiers using a newly-curated, high quality dataset. Results indicate that the instruction-based classifier outperforms encoder-based ones and gains from comparison with reference images. This shows that IDC can be an effective task modeling for tackling data constraints in infrastructure inspection and DT asset condition updating.

</details>


### [203] [Humans' ALMANAC: A Human Collaboration Dataset of Action-Level Mental Model Annotations for Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06388)
*Jiaju Chen,Yuxuan Lu,Jiayi Su,Chaoran Chen,Songlin Xiao,Zheng Zhang,Yun Wang,Yunyao Li,Jian Zhao,Tongshuang Wu,Toby Jia-Jun Li,Dakuo Wang,Bingsheng Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ALMANAC数据集，包含2,987个带心理模型标注的协作动作，用于提升LLM智能体的过程级协作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体主要针对任务完成优化，缺乏在协作过程中持续维护和对齐心理模型（自我推理、伙伴意图、共享目标）的能力，且社区缺少带有动作级心理模型标注的真实人类协作数据。

Method: 基于社会科学中的Map Task双人路由任务，构建了包含2,987个协作动作的数据集，每个动作都配有理论指导的心理模型标注（自我推理、感知伙伴意图、感知团队目标），并评估了六个LLM在预测人类下一轮行为和心理模型上的表现。

Result: 六个LLM的基准测试结果展示了ALMANAC数据集在评估模型模拟人类协作行为及推断其潜在心理模型方面的实用性。

Conclusion: ALMANAC数据集能够有效帮助评估和引导LLM智能体发展过程级的协作能力，弥补了当前智能体在协作心理模型对齐方面的不足。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM agents have enabled complex cognitive capabilities, such as multi-step reasoning, planning, and tool use, that increasingly position these agents as human collaborators. Effective collaboration, however, requires collaborators to continuously maintain and align mental models of their own reasoning,partners' intentions, and shared goals during the collaborative process. Today's agents rarely develop such capabilities since they are primarily optimized for task completion, and the community lacks authentic human collaboration data with action-level mental model annotations that could guide agents toward process-level collaborative competence. To bridge this gap, we present ALMANAC, a dataset of Action-Level Mental model ANnotations for Agent Collaboration built from the Map Task, a classic dyadic routing task from social science. ALMANAC contains 2,987 collaboration actions, each paired with theory-informed mental model annotations that record the participants' self-reasoning, perceived partner intent, and perceived team goal. We benchmark six LLMs on predicting humans' next-turn behavior and mental models. Our results demonstrate ALMANAC's utility in evaluating models' ability to simulate human collaborative behaviors and infer their underlying mental models.

</details>


### [204] [Risk Assessment of Autonomous Driving: Integrating Technical Failures, Ethical Dilemmas, and Policy Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06396)
*Boyi Chen,Shengqin Chu,Zicheng Wang,Brian Baetz,Zhen Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于NHTSA、DMV、MIT道德机器数据集及五个司法管辖区法规分析，发现自动驾驶主要技术故障是感知和分类错误，且存在伦理框架差异和法规不一致问题，建议采用适应性协作治理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶技术虽能减少人为事故，但带来技术、伦理和法规方面的新型风险，需综合评估。

Method: 分析NHTSA事故数据、DMV脱离报告、MIT道德机器数据集，并对五个司法管辖区进行法规比较。

Result: 主要技术故障模式为感知和分类错误，事故占比较高；不同地区存在伦理框架差异，法规不一致增加了应用不确定性。

Conclusion: 技术、伦理和法规问题紧密相关，需协同解决；建议采用结合工程标准、伦理讨论和制度监管的适应性协作治理方法。

Abstract: Autonomous driving technology has the potential to reduce the large number of road traffic accidents caused by human error each year, but it also brings new types of risks that need to be evaluated from the aspects of technology, ethics and regulations. Based on public crash data from the National Highway Traffic Safety Administration (NHTSA), disengagement reports from the California Department of Motor Vehicles (DMV), the MIT Moral Machines dataset, and a comparative regulatory analysis of five jurisdictions, we have found that the main types of technical failure modes are perception and classification errors. These account for a relatively large proportion of the reported accidents, and it can be concluded that there are different ethical frameworks for autonomous vehicle decision-making, and inconsistent regulations in different areas increase the uncertainty of widespread application. Generally speaking, the problems of technology, ethics and regulation are closely related and need to be solved together. Therefore, this paper recommends a more adaptive and cooperative governance approach that combines engineering standards, ethical discussion, and institutional supervision.

</details>


### [205] [Unsupervised Skill Discovery for Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06416)
*Zhisong Qiu,Kangqi Song,Shengwei Tang,Shuofei Qiao,Lei Liang,Huajun Chen,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DataCOPE框架，通过无监督的验证器引导技能发现，在不更新模型参数的情况下提升数据分析代理的表现。在报告型分析和推理型分析任务上分别提升了9.71%和32.30%的平均分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据分析中有效的技能发现很困难，因为可靠的监督信号成本高，且不同分析格式的成功标准各异。核心问题是如何仅从无标签的探索中自动发现可复用的数据分析技能。

Method: 提出DataCOPE框架，包含三个组件：数据分析代理（生成轨迹）、无监督验证器（提取信号）和技能管理器（对比式技能蒸馏）。针对报告型分析实例化为自适应检查表验证器，针对推理型分析实例化为答案一致性验证器。

Result: 在Deep Data Research（报告型）和DABStep（推理型）两项任务上，DataCOPE持续优于基线方法。平均提升幅度为报告型9.71%，推理型32.30%。

Conclusion: DataCOPE通过无监督验证器引导的技能发现，能够有效提取可复用的数据分析技能，显著提升代理在多种分析格式下的表现。

Abstract: Inference-time skill augmentation provides a lightweight way to improve data-analytic agents by injecting reusable procedural knowledge without updating model parameters. However, discovering effective skills for data analysis remains challenging, as reliable supervision is expensive and success criteria vary across analytical formats. This raises the key question of how to discover reusable data-analysis skills from unlabeled exploration alone. We propose DataCOPE, an unsupervised verifier-guided skill discovery framework for data-analytic agents. DataCOPE derives verifier signals from the exploration trajectories and uses them to characterize relative quality or aggreement among trajectories. It iteratively coordinates a Data-Analytic Agent for trajectory generation, an Unsupervised Verifier for signal extraction, and a Skill Manager for contrastive skill distillation. For report-style analysis, we instantiate the verifier as an Adaptive Checklist Verifier that derives task-specific criteria, scores reports by verifiable coverage, and iteratively refines the checklist. For reasoning-style analysis, we instantiate it as an Answer Agreement Verifier that groups trajectories by answer agreement and uses self-consistency as an auxiliary signal. We evaluate DataCOPE on report-style analysis from Deep Data Research and reasoning-style analysis from DABStep. Across both settings, DataCOPE consistently improves held-out performance over baselines. Averaged across four model settings, DataCOPE improves the mean score by 9.71% and 32.30% on report-style and reasoning-style tasks respectively.

</details>


### [206] [Agent Memory: Characterization and System Implications of Stateful Long-Horizon Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06448)
*Yasmine Omri,Ziyu Gan,Zachary Broveak,Robin Geens,Zexue He,Alex Pentland,Marian Verhelst,Tsachy Weissman,Thierry Tambe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM智能体记忆系统进行系统性表征分析，提出了面向系统的分类法、阶段感知分析框架，并对10种代表性系统进行了基准测试，最终给出10条系统设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长时任务中需要持续存储、检索和更新记忆，但目前各类记忆系统的系统级行为尚未被充分刻画，缺乏统一的理解框架。

Method: 提出四轴系统导向分类法；构建阶段感知性能分析工具，将成本分解为构建、检索和生成三个阶段；在两类基准套件上对10个代表性系统进行表征。

Result: 揭示了不同设计选择如何在读写路径上分配成本，包括构建调度、能力下限、查询量摊销、新鲜度-延迟权衡及集群管理等方面的权衡。

Conclusion: 给出了10条系统设计建议，涵盖构建调度、能力下限、查询量摊销、新鲜度-延迟权衡以及集群规模管理，为构建高效智能体记忆系统提供指导。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed on long-horizon tasks requiring sustained reasoning over extended interaction histories. Realizing this at scale requires agents to persistently store, retrieve, and update their own memory across sessions. A rich ecosystem of agent memory systems has emerged spanning flat retrieval, LLM-mediated extraction, consolidating fact stores, and agentic control flows. Yet, their system-level behavior remains uncharacterized. We present the first systems characterization of agent memory. First, we introduce a system-oriented taxonomy classifying agent memory systems along four axes. Second, we build a phase-aware profiling harness attributing cost to construction, retrieval, and generation. Third, we characterize ten representative systems across two benchmark suites, uncovering how design choices shift cost across the write and read paths. Finally, we derive 10 system recommendations covering construction scheduling, capability floors, amortization via query volume, freshness-latency tradeoffs, and fleet-scale management.

</details>


### [207] [Vortex: Efficient and Programmable Sparse Attention Serving for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06453)
*Zhuoming Chen,Xinrui Zhong,Qilong Feng,Ranajoy Sadhukhan,Yang Zhou,Michael Qizhe Shieh,Zhihao Jia,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Vortex是一个结合Python嵌入式前端语言和页面中心张量抽象的系统，用于高效表达、快速原型设计、部署和评估各种稀疏注意力算法，能显著提升大语言模型推理吞吐量（最高达4.7倍），并支持AI代理自动生成和优化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型生成长度不断增长，稀疏注意力变得越来越重要，但大规模部署和评估新稀疏注意力算法在工程上非常复杂且耗时，严重制约了人类研究人员和AI代理在稀疏注意力设计领域的探索效率。

Method: Vortex采用Python嵌入的前端语言，基于页面中心的张量抽象来表达广泛的稀疏注意力算法；后端紧密集成到现代LLM服务堆栈中，实现高效的部署和执行。

Result: AI代理使用Vortex自动生成和优化算法，最优算法相比全注意力吞吐量提升最高达3.46倍；在MLA-based GLM-4.7-Flash上达到4.7倍提速，在229B参数的MiniMax-M2.7上达到1.37倍提速（NVIDIA B200 GPU）。

Conclusion: Vortex有效加速了稀疏注意力算法的设计和迭代，将理论效率转化为实际吞吐量提升，并扩展了稀疏注意力在新兴架构和超大规模模型上的应用。

Abstract: Sparse attention is becoming increasingly important for serving large language models (LLMs) as generation lengths continue to grow. However, deploying and evaluating new sparse attention algorithms at scale remains highly engineering-intensive, slowing both human researchers and AI agents in exploring the sparse attention design. To address this challenge, we present Vortex, a system that combines a Python-embedded frontend language atop a page-centric tensor abstraction for expressing a broad range of sparse attention algorithms, with an efficient backend tightly integrated into modern LLM serving stacks. Vortex enables rapid prototyping, deployment, and evaluation of sparse attention algorithms, effectively translating their theoretical efficiency gains into real-world throughput improvements. As a result, Vortex substantially accelerates the design and iteration of sparse attention algorithms. First, AI agents use Vortex to automatically generate and refine diverse algorithms, the best reaching up to $3.46\times$ higher throughput than full attention while preserving accuracy. Second, Vortex extends sparse attention to emerging architectures and very large models that are otherwise hard to experiment with, reaching up to $4.7\times$ higher throughput on the MLA-based GLM-4.7-Flash and $1.37\times$ on the 229B-parameter MiniMax-M2.7 on NVIDIA B200 GPUs.

</details>


### [208] [Benchmark Everything Everywhere All at Once](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06462)
*Shiyun Xiong,Dongming Wu,Peiwen Sun,Yuang Ai,Bokang Yang,Wencheng Han,Xiao-Hui Li,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Benchmark Agent，一个全自动的智能体系统，能够从用户查询分析到数据标注和质量控制的完整流程中自动构建基准测试，解决了传统基准构建劳动密集、可复用性差和性能饱和等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准测试构建劳动密集、难以复用，可持续性和可扩展性堪忧；且现有基准发布后很快达到性能饱和，无法有效区分最先进模型。

Method: 设计了一个全自动的智能体系统(Benchmark Agent)，涵盖用户查询分析、子任务设计、数据标注和质量控制等完整基准构建流程。

Result: 生成了15个代表性基准测试，涵盖文本理解、多模态理解和领域特定推理；通过人工评估、LLM-as-a-judge评估和一致性检查证明其能生成高质量样本且只需极少人工参与；还发现当前模型在某些领域特定推理任务上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 快速演进的基准测试对研究社区有重要贡献，该系统将在demo页面和代码仓库公开。

Abstract: Benchmarks are fundamental for evaluating and advancing LLMs and MLLMs by providing standardized and explicit measures of performance. However, their construction is labor-intensive and hard to reuse, raising concerns about sustainability and scalability. Moreover, existing benchmarks often quickly reach performance saturation after their release, resulting in insufficient discrimination among state-of-the-art models. To address these challenges, we introduce Benchmark Agent, a fully autonomous agentic system designed for benchmark building. Our framework orchestrates the complete benchmark construction pipeline, from user query analysis and subtask design to data annotation and quality control. To assess Benchmark Agent, we implement it to produce 15 representative benchmarks, spanning diverse evaluation scenarios, including text understanding, multimodal understanding, and domain-specific reasoning. Extensive experiments, including human evaluation, LLM-as-a-judge assessment, and consistency checks, demonstrate Benchmark Agent can generate high-quality benchmark samples with minimal human involvement. More importantly, through continual evaluation, we observe several insightful findings, including that current models struggle with certain domain-specific reasoning tasks. We believe that rapidly evolving benchmarks can contribute significantly to the research community. The preview and code will be publicly available at the demo page and code repository.

</details>


### [209] [Goedel-Architect: Streamlining Formal Theorem Proving with Blueprint Generation and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06468)
*Jui-Hui Chung,Ziyang Cai,Zihao Li,Qishuo Yin,Rohit Agarwal,Simon Park,Rodrigo Porto,Narutatsu Ri,Ziran Yang,Shange Tang,Xingyu Dang,Hongzhou Lin,Mengdi Wang,Danqi Chen,Chi Jin,Liam H Fowl,Sanjeev Arora*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Goedel-Architect是一个面向Lean 4的定理证明智能体框架，通过蓝图生成和迭代细化策略，在多个数学竞赛基准上取得了SOTA性能，成本比同类开源管线低500倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化定理证明方法（如递归引理分解）容易陷入低效循环和死胡同策略，缺乏全局视野。本文旨在提出一种基于蓝图（依赖图）的全局规划与迭代细化框架，提升定理证明的效率和成功率。

Method: 1) 首先生成包含形式化定义、引理及依赖关系的蓝图（可选由自然语言证明引导）；2) 配备工具的Lean 4证明器并行地利用相关依赖证明每个未闭合的引理节点；3) 失败的引理驱动全局蓝图的细化和迭代。

Result: 以DeepSeek-V4-Flash为骨干，在MiniF2F-test上达到99.2% pass@1（加自然语言证明可达100%），PutnamBench上达到75.6% pass@1（加自然语言证明可达88.8%），并在IMO 2025、Putnam 2025、USAMO 2026等竞赛上取得优异结果。

Conclusion: Goedel-Architect通过蓝图生成与迭代细化策略实现了当前开源管线的SOTA性能，且成本显著低于同类系统（最高可达500倍）。

Abstract: We introduce Goedel-Architect, an agentic framework for formal theorem proving in Lean 4 centered on blueprint generation and refinement. A blueprint is a dependency graph of definitions and lemmas that builds up to the main theorem. First, Goedel-Architect generates a blueprint of formally stated definitions and lemmas, along with declared dependencies. This blueprint is optionally guided by a natural language proof. Then, a tool-equipped Lean prover component closes each open lemma node in parallel using relevant dependencies. Failed lemmas in turn drive refinement of the global blueprint. This strategy contrasts with other mainstream approaches which use recursive lemma decomposition, and can inefficiently loop on dead-end strategies. Using the open-weight DeepSeek-V4-Flash (284B-A13B) as the backbone, Goedel-Architect attains 99.2% pass@1 on MiniF2F-test and 75.6% pass@1 on PutnamBench. With an optional natural-language proof seeding the initial blueprint on the harder problems, we additionally close the remaining two MiniF2F-test problems (reaching 100%), lift PutnamBench to 88.8% (597/672), and solve 4/6 on IMO 2025, 11/12 on Putnam 2025, and 3/6 on USAMO 2026. This represents state-of-the-art performance for an open-source pipeline at a price point up to 500x less than comparable open-source pipelines.

</details>


### [210] [MLEvolve: A Self-Evolving Framework for Automated Machine Learning Algorithm Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06473)
*Shangheng Du,Xiangchao Yan,Jinxin Shi,Zongsheng Cao,Shiyang Feng,Zichen Liang,Boyuan Sun,Tianshuo Peng,Yifan Zhou,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MLEvolve是一个基于LLM的自我进化多智能体框架，通过渐进式MCGS、回溯记忆和分层架构，在MLE-Bench上达到SOTA性能，并超越专业算法发现方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLE智能体存在分支间信息隔离、无记忆搜索和缺乏分层控制的问题，限制了长期优化的能力。

Method: 1) 扩展树搜索为Progressive MCGS，通过图参考边实现跨分支信息流动，并用熵启发式渐进调度从探索转向利用；2) 引入回溯记忆（冷启动知识库+动态全局记忆）实现经验积累和复用；3) 解耦策略规划与代码生成，采用自适应编码模式。

Result: 在MLE-Bench上，12小时预算内平均奖牌率和有效提交率均达SOTA；在数学算法优化任务上超越AlphaEvolve等专业方法，展示出强跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: MLEvolve通过跨分支信息融合、经验记忆与分层控制有效解决了长期优化难题，验证了LLM智能体在ML算法发现中的自我进化潜力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to long-horizon tasks such as scientific discovery and machine learning engineering (MLE), where sustained self-evolution becomes a key capability. However, existing MLE agents suffer from inter-branch information isolation, memoryless search, and lack of hierarchical control, which together hinder long-horizon optimization. We present MLEvolve, an LLM-based self-evolving multi-agent framework for end-to-end machine learning algorithm discovery. By extending tree search to Progressive MCGS, MLEvolve enables cross-branch information flow through graph-based reference edges and gradually shifts the search from broad exploration to focused exploitation with an entropy-inspired progressive schedule. To allow the agent to evolve with accumulated experience, we introduce Retrospective Memory, which combines a cold-start domain knowledge base with a dynamic global memory for task-specific experience retrieval and reuse. For stable long-horizon iteration, we further decouple strategic planning from code generation with adaptive coding modes. Evaluation on MLE-Bench shows that MLEvolve achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple dimensions including average medal rate and valid submission rate under a 12-hour budget (half the standard runtime). Moreover, MLEvolve also outperforms specialized algorithm discovery methods including AlphaEvolve on mathematical algorithm optimization tasks, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/InternScience/MLEvolve.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [The Evaluation Blind Spot: A Stereological Theory of Benchmark Coverage for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05169)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLM基准测试的体视学覆盖理论，发现主流排行榜的有效维度d_eff在2.86-4.80之间，结构盲点比分数差距大两个数量级；仅需4-7个基准即可实现稳定覆盖，并解决了Gardner关于C^2支撑函数的极值恢复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准测试缺乏严格的理论分析，无法量化基准套件的覆盖完备性及其盲区大小，亟需建立数学框架来理解评估体系的可靠性与冗余性。

Method: 建立凸能力廓线的体视学覆盖理论，推导Hausdorff距离的上下界；通过主成分分析计算有效维度；采用卡方投影模型模拟排名翻转；利用子模贪心算法寻找基准核心子集；通过反事实验证评估特征结构对基准不可替代性的预测能力。

Result: 三个排行榜有效维度均在2.86-4.80之间；结构盲点远超统计噪声（52-127倍）；模拟排名翻转率稳定在0.38-0.49；92%的随机划分导致榜首交换；4个基准构成稳定核心，7个即可覆盖90%；特征结构可预测基准的不可替代性（rho=-0.69）和新信息引入能力（rho=+0.38）。

Conclusion: LLM基准测试存在显著的结构性盲区，排名对基准选择高度敏感；通过体视学理论可系统性地识别核心基准并量化评估完备性；同时解决了Gardner的极值恢复问题，建立了更普适的理论框架。

Abstract: We give a stereological theory of LLM benchmark coverage. For any suite with effective dimensionality d_eff, the visible Hausdorff distance between two convex capability profiles consistent with the same scores is bounded by epsilon + C R m^(-1/(d_eff-1)), with matching Lipschitz lower bound. Empirically, three independent leaderboards (Open LLM v2, an extended 12-benchmark suite, LiveBench) all have d_eff in [2.86, 4.80] on their competitive frontier; the structural blind spot exceeds the observed runner-up score gap by two orders of magnitude and dominates statistical noise by 52-127x. Under a chi-squared projection model, the isotropic prior is the optimistic case; across six hidden-capability priors and four ambient dimensions the simulated half-split swap rate of the top two models stays in [0.38, 0.49], and a 500-trial random visible/held-out split shows that 92% of trials swap the top-1 ranking with on average 2.83 of 5 top-5 models changing. A submodular greedy algorithm with the Nemhauser (1 - 1/e) guarantee finds a stable core of 4 benchmarks; 7 of 12 suffice for 90% coverage, and the trained subset transfers across temporal quarters with 93-97% retention. A counterfactual validation across 12 internal benchmarks and 27 Chatbot Arena categories confirms that the eigenstructure predicts which evaluations are irreplaceable (rho = -0.69, p = 0.013 for removal disruption) and which external evaluations bring new information (rho = +0.38). As a second, independent theoretical contribution, we resolve Gardner's Problem 1.5 (1995) for C^2 support functions, establishing the minimax rate Theta(R/(kappa m^(2/(D-1)))) in general dimension via optimal recovery theory on S^(D-1).

</details>


### [212] [ERRORQUAKE: Heavy-Tailed Error Severity Distributions in Open-Weight Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05170)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Errorquake-10k基准，发现即使准确率相同，不同开源LLM的错误严重程度分布差异显著，并证明错误率与严重度分布信息非冗余，建议报告严重度分布作为准确率的补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准将所有错误等同对待（如错误日期与虚构法庭裁决视为同等），忽略了错误严重程度的巨大差异，而标量错误率无法反映这一差异。

Method: 构建10,000条查询的Errorquake-10k基准，采用0-4连续严重度量表，覆盖8个领域和5个难度层级；拟合21个开源模型的严重度分布，计算Gutenberg-Richter上尾斜率b及95%置信区间；通过519项三标注者人类验证研究确认可靠性；证明非冗余定理。

Result: 210个模型对中有85对在匹配准确率下b的95%置信区间不重叠；严重度分布与错误率的互信息为1.56比特，64.5%的模型间b方差无法由错误率解释；低严重度错误71%为检索错误，高严重度错误39%为虚构。

Conclusion: 严重度分布包含错误率无法承载的区分性信息，应作为准确率的补充指标一同报告。

Abstract: At matched accuracy, open-weight LLMs differ substantially in the shape of their error severity distribution -- a difference invisible to the scalar error rate. Hallucination benchmarks report a single error count and treat all errors as equivalent, yet a wrong date and a fabricated court ruling differ by orders of magnitude. We introduce Errorquake-10k, a 10,000-query benchmark scoring each response on a continuous 0-4 severity scale across 8 domains and 5 difficulty tiers, and we fit per-model severity distributions for 21 open-weight models. For each model we estimate a severity distribution index (b, the Gutenberg-Richter upper-tail slope) with 95% bootstrap confidence intervals. Headline: across the 210 model pairs, 85 have disjoint 95% b confidence intervals at matched accuracy (|Delta epsilon| < 0.05) on human-consensus scoring, e.g. deepseek-v3.2 vs. ministral-14b at epsilon = 0.586 and Delta b = 0.47. A 519-item three-rater human validation study confirms measurement reliability (ICC(2,k=3) = 0.85), validates the LLM-judge ranking (rho = 0.89), and confirms the dense-model scaling correlation on human data (rho_s = -0.86). We prove a Non-Reducibility Theorem showing that severity profile and error rate are informationally non-redundant (I(b; model | epsilon) = 1.56 bits; 64.5% of cross-model b variance is unexplained by epsilon). A severity mechanism taxonomy (kappa = 0.83) reveals that error type shifts categorically with severity: low-severity errors are retrievals (71%); high-severity errors are fabrications (39%) -- and this composition differs by model size (p < 0.0001). Severity distribution should be reported alongside accuracy; it carries discriminative information that the error rate cannot.

</details>


### [213] [Staged Factorial Screening for Budget-Constrained Micro-Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05186)
*Felipe Chavarro Polania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在预算受限的微预训练场景中，通过分阶段分数阶因子设计来识别早期有效结构。在613次实验后发现，总批次、深度和宽度等主要惩罚在短预算下最大，随预算增加而放松。最终提出了一个有限方法：使用短设计筛选高惩罚方向，确认有希望的锚点，并在缩减空间内局部优化。证据支持以桥接模型为中心的推荐，但并非硬件无关排名或通用超参数优化优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预算受限的微预训练中，常常需要在共享加速器上对大量候选配方进行筛选，然后才投入更大的搜索预算。如何在这类受限条件下高效、稳定地识别早期有效因子结构是一个关键问题。

Method: 采用分阶段分数阶因子设计工作流程，在固定单GPU训练循环上运行613次实验，涵盖不同时间长度（2/5/10分钟）的试点和后续筛选、全条件种子重运行、目标种子锚点检查、贪婪和随机基线、桥接包以及多主机锚点延续实验。使用Benjamini-Hochberg校正进行显著性分析。

Result: 总批次、深度和宽度在短预算下惩罚最大，随预算增加而放松。在预声明的种子全屏幕家族中，D、A、B、C在5和10分钟后保持非零估计，E则没有。随机搜索能找到强候选但缺乏因子归因。桥接锚点具有最低均值，但无法分离工作流改进与模型容量优势。在12小时和24小时延续中，桥接模型最低，非桥接排序受主机影响。

Conclusion: 提出了一个有限方法：用短设计筛选高惩罚方向，通过重复运行确认有希望的锚点，在缩减空间内局部优化。证据支持以桥接模型为中心的推荐，但并非硬件无关排名或通用超参数优化优势。

Abstract: Budget-constrained micro-pretraining often requires triaging many candidate recipes on a shared accelerator before larger search budgets are spent. We study whether a staged fractional-factorial workflow can recover stable early effect structure in this setting. On a fixed autoresearch-derived single-GPU training loop, we run 613 experiments across pilot and follow-up screens at 2, 5, and 10 minutes; full 16-condition seeded reruns at 5 and 10 minutes; targeted seeded anchor checks; same-host greedy and matched-cost random baselines; a 60-minute bridge package; and bounded Windows A100 and Linux L40S anchor continuations through 24 hours. Main penalties from total batch, depth, and width are largest at short budgets and relax as budget increases. Within the predeclared seeded full-screen families, D, A, B, and C retain non-zero estimates at 5 and 10 minutes after within-budget Benjamini-Hochberg correction, while E does not. Random search can reach strong incumbents in this 32-condition space, but repeatedly in the same low-penalty region and without factor attribution. The 60-minute bridge anchor has the lowest mean, although that package does not separate workflow refinement from the larger bridge model's capacity advantage. In bounded 12-hour and 24-hour three-anchor continuations on both hosts, the bridge has the lowest sample mean while the non-bridge ordering stays host-sensitive. We therefore present a bounded methods result: use short designed screens to identify high-penalty directions, confirm promising anchors under repeated runs, and refine locally inside the reduced space. The evidence supports a bridge-centered recommendation through 24 hours on two hosts, not hardware-invariant ranking or general hyperparameter-optimization superiority.

</details>


### [214] [State commitment learning: training language models to distinguish computation from memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05201)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出状态承诺学习（State Commitment Learning）和反事实擦除强化学习（CERL），训练模型区分临时计算与持久状态，减少推理对隐藏思路的依赖，在数学、长链逻辑等任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理语言模型将所有生成的token（包括失败尝试、死胡同、临时草稿）都保留在上下文中，导致后续推理可能依赖这些本不应依赖的信息，缺乏对"持久状态"与"临时计算"的明确区分。

Method: 提出状态承诺学习框架，定义持久状态充分性（persistent-state sufficiency）作为反事实标准；提出CERL方法，在同一前缀下对比保留与擦除隐藏思路两条路径，仅当擦除路径仍正确时给予奖励。

Result: 在数学、长链逻辑、科学QA、多轮工具使用任务中，CERL显著降低答案对隐藏思路的依赖，同时保持准确性，优于仅使用正确性RL和长答案SFT基线。

Conclusion: 通过明确区分临时计算与持久状态，可以有效减少模型推理对隐藏思路的不当依赖，提升推理鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reasoning language models do not distinguish tokens used for computation from tokens that constitute persistent state: once generated, all hidden thoughts remain in context and influence future predictions. As a result, downstream reasoning may depend on failed attempts, dead ends, and private scratch work that should not be safely relied on later. We recast this phenomenon as a new training objective, state commitment learning: training models to explicitly distinguish information that should be committed as persistent state from temporary computation that can be discarded. We define a counterfactual criterion, persistent-state sufficiency, which makes it trainable and measurable whether an answer remains usable after hidden thoughts are erased. We then propose Counterfactual Erasure RL (CERL), which evaluates, under the same prefix, both a path that keeps hidden thoughts and a path that erases them, and gives reward only when the erasure path remains correct. We also introduce the Erasure Dependence Protocol and show across mathematics, long-chain logic, scientific QA, and multi-turn tool-use evaluation that CERL substantially reduces answer dependence on hidden thoughts without sacrificing accuracy, consistently outperforming correctness-only RL and long-answer SFT baselines.

</details>


### [215] [Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Restores Symmetry in Deep Linear Networks with Multi-Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05219)
*Hee-Sung Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度线性网络中，大步长离散梯度下降（GD）如何通过边缘稳定性（Edge of Stability）的振荡效应，打破早期深度驱动的对称性破缺，促使信号在多个路径间重新分配，从而避免单一路径主导的“赢家通吃”现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通过梯度流（GF）预测多路径深度线性网络会出现“赢家通吃”的专业化现象，但离散梯度下降（GD）在大步长下的行为尚不明确，本文旨在揭示大步长GD如何影响路径竞争与信号分配。

Method: 理论证明单路径解是尖锐极小值，而多路径分配能降低尖锐度；通过分析边缘稳定性下的振荡行为，解释早期与后期训练阶段的转变机制。

Result: 大步长GD在早期重现深度驱动的对称性破缺，但随后边缘稳定性引发的振荡会覆盖这一趋势，驱动网络进入重新平衡阶段，使信号在多路径间重新分配。

Conclusion: 深度影响路径竞争，大步长GD倾向于共享表示而非单路径主导，这为理解深度学习中的隐式正则化与表示学习提供了新视角。

Abstract: Recent analyses of multi-pathway Deep Linear Networks use Gradient Flow to predict a "winner-takes-all" specialization in which path symmetry breaks and each feature concentrates in a single pathway. In this work, we show that discrete Gradient Descent (GD) with a large step size tells a different story. We prove that single-path solutions are sharp minima, whereas distributing signals across pathways reduces sharpness by a factor that decreases with both the number of pathways and depth. Consequently, while early training reproduces the depth-driven symmetry breaking predicted by GF, oscillations at the Edge of Stability subsequently override this tendency and drive the network into a re-balancing phase, where signals redistribute across pathways. Together, these results clarify how depth shapes pathway competition and explain why large-step GD favors shared representations rather than persistent single-pathway dominance.

</details>


### [216] [Differentiable Efficient Operator Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05232)
*Xiaohuan Pei,Jiyuan Zhang,Yuanfan Guo,Weiguo Feng,Tao Huang,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可微的算子搜索框架（EOS），能够自动搜索多模态基础模型中的token缩减策略，包括在哪里缩减、保留多少token以及如何处理缩减后的信息，在视觉token大幅压缩下实现更好的精度-效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态基础模型依赖手工设计的token缩减算子（如剪枝、合并、池化、自适应重加权），这些算子看似不同但缺乏统一视角，且手动设计无法充分探索混合算子空间。

Method: 提出Efficient Operator Search（EOS）框架，将token缩减算子统一到一个共享算子空间中，并通过可微搜索联合优化三个维度：层激活位置、保留预算（token数量）和算子行为（如何聚合信息），在单边预算与成本约束下优化任务性能。

Result: 实验表明，搜索得到的算子能够复现手工设计的基线方法，并发现超越孤立手工设计的混合算子，在多项多模态基准测试上取得竞争性的精度-效率权衡，尤其在激进的视觉token缩减场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 高效多模态推理可以从手工算子设计转向可微算子搜索，这为自动化token缩减提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Efficient multimodal foundation models often rely on manually designed token-reduction operators, such as pruning, merging, pooling, and adaptive reweighting. Although these operators appear different, we show that they can be interpreted as distinct regimes of a shared operator space. Based on this view, we introduce Efficient Operator Search, a differentiable framework that jointly searches where to reduce tokens, how many tokens to retain, and how reduced token information should be processed. The proposed search space parameterizes layer activation, retention budget, and operator behavior, while the search policy optimizes task performance under one-sided budget and cost constraints. This formulation recovers representative hand-designed baselines as special cases and further discovers hybrid operators beyond isolated manual designs. Experiments on multimodal benchmarks show that the searched operators achieve competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs, especially under aggressive visual-token reduction. These results suggest that efficient multimodal inference can be reframed from manual operator design to differentiable operator search.

</details>


### [217] [DiffSlack: Learning under Nonlinear Inequality Constraints via Learnable Slack Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05247)
*Ziqian Wang,Chenxi Fang,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffSlack，一种可微投影层，通过可学习的松弛变量和阻尼高斯-牛顿投影，将非线性不等式约束嵌入神经网络，在车辆路径规划中取得了更好的约束满足和规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在神经网络中强制执行非线性不等式约束仍然具有挑战性，尤其是当输出受到多个耦合约束时。现有的硬约束方法往往对约束集施加结构限制，或为大规模非线性问题引入大量计算开销。

Method: DiffSlack将不等式重新表述为带有可学习松弛变量的等式，这些变量作为增广网络输出的一部分进行预测，为阻尼高斯-牛顿投影提供数据驱动的热启动。投影层将原始预测映射到增广可行流形上，同时保持端到端可微性。两阶段课程学习进一步稳定训练并改善约束满足。

Result: 在包含200个非线性不等式约束（碰撞避免、曲率限制、路径点间距）的车辆路径规划任务中，DiffSlack相比现有基线方法，在可比推理预算下实现了更高的规划成功率和更强的几何约束满足。消融实验表明硬投影层降低了对监督质量的敏感性。CARLA和真实车辆实验验证了轨迹的可执行性。

Conclusion: DiffSlack为工程应用中将硬不等式约束嵌入神经网络提供了一种实用且可扩展的方法。

Abstract: Enforcing nonlinear inequality constraints in neural networks remains challenging, especially when the output is subject to many coupled constraints. Existing hard constraint methods often impose structural restrictions on the constraint set or introduce substantial computational overhead for large-scale nonlinear problems. Here, we propose DiffSlack, a differentiable projection layer for nonlinear inequality-constrained neural prediction. DiffSlack reformulates inequalities as equalities with learnable slack variables, which are predicted as part of the augmented network output and provide a data-driven warm start for damped Gauss-Newton projection. The projection layer maps raw predictions onto the augmented feasible manifold while preserving end-to-end differentiability. A two-stage curriculum further stabilizes training and improves constraint satisfaction. We evaluate DiffSlack on vehicle path planning with 200 nonlinear inequality constraints from collision avoidance, curvature limits, and waypoint spacing. Compared with existing learning-based baselines, DiffSlack achieves a higher planning success rate and stronger geometric constraint satisfaction under a comparable inference budget. Ablation studies further show that the hard projection layer reduces sensitivity to supervision quality. Closed-loop tracking in CARLA and real-world vehicle experiments confirms the executability of the generated trajectories. These results demonstrate that DiffSlack provides a practical and scalable approach to embedding hard inequality constraints into neural networks for engineering applications.

</details>


### [218] [Alpha-RTL: Test-Time Training for RTL Hardware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05253)
*Peilong Zhou,Zhirong Chen,Cangyuan Li,Haoyu Gao,Kaiyan Chang,Ziming Qu,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TTT-RTL，首个在测试时针对具体RTL设计进行强化学习的框架，通过EDA反馈（语法检查、仿真、综合PPA）在线更新LLM策略，在RTLLM v2.0上将PPA乘积降低65.1%，优于冻结策略基线（26.1%），并在工业级C910 FPU单元上实现59.4%的ADP降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成RTL的方法要么训练通用生成器后直接部署，要么在测试时用冻结策略进行搜索，无法针对具体设计问题利用EDA反馈自适应优化。作者希望让LLM在测试时也能根据可执行的EDA反馈进行策略更新，从而生成物理优化的硬件设计。

Method: TTT-RTL框架包含：(1) 采样候选实现并通过语法检查和仿真验证；(2) 对有效设计用综合得出的PPA乘积评分；(3) 通过PUCT索引的设计状态池重用高奖励变体；(4) 用熵策略梯度目标更新策略。引入自适应KL预算控制器，根据参考KL、有效样本量和奖励饱和信号调整熵约束，稳定策略更新。

Result: 在RTLLM v2.0 (Nangate 45nm)上，TTT-RTL将几何平均PPA乘积降低65.1%，最强冻结策略基线仅26.1%。在工业级XuanTie C910 FPU前导零预测单元(Sky130)上实现59.4%的ADP降低。消融实验证实策略自适应、状态重用和KL预算控制均有贡献。

Conclusion: 测试时训练结合可执行EDA反馈能使LLM的RTL生成超越功能正确性，迈向物理优化的硬件设计，表明该范式在硬件设计自动化领域具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown increasing promise in generating
  functionally correct register-transfer-level (RTL) hardware designs.
  Recent systems improve further through EDA-integrated reinforcement
  learning with syntax, simulation, and PPA rewards, but train a general
  RTL generator before deployment while test-time approaches search with
  a frozen policy. We instead perform reinforcement learning at test time,
  allowing the LLM policy to adapt to executable EDA feedback for the
  specific RTL problem at hand. We propose TTT-RTL, to our knowledge the
  first per-design test-time training framework that closes the loop
  between an LLM policy and an EDA pipeline for RTL optimization. TTT-RTL
  samples candidate implementations, verifies them through syntax checking
  and simulation, scores valid designs using synthesis-derived PPA product,
  reuses high-reward variants through a PUCT-indexed design-state pool,
  and updates the policy with an entropic policy-gradient objective. To
  stabilize policy updates under sparse or plateaued rewards, we introduce
  an adaptive KL-budget controller that adjusts the entropy constraint
  using reference KL, effective sample size, and reward saturation signals.
  On RTLLM v2.0 under Nangate 45nm, TTT-RTL reduces the geometric-mean
  PPA product by 65.1% over the reference, outperforming the strongest
  published frozen-policy agent baseline at 26.1%. On an industrial
  XuanTie C910 FPU leading-zero-anticipation unit under Sky130, TTT-RTL
  achieves a 59.4% ADP reduction, and ablations confirm that policy
  adaptation, state reuse, and KL-budget control each contribute. These
  results suggest that test-time training with executable EDA feedback can
  move LLM-based RTL generation beyond functional correctness toward
  physically optimized hardware.

</details>


### [219] [Flash-WAM: Modality-Aware Distillation for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05254)
*Arman Akbari,Ci Zhang,Arash Akbari,Lin Zhao,Yixiao Chen,Weiwei Chen,Xuan Zhang,Geng Yuan,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flash-WAM提出了一种模态感知的步骤蒸馏框架，针对世界-动作模型中视频和动作流使用不同噪声调度的问题，通过为每种模态匹配不同的参数化方式，将推理压缩到单步，实现23倍加速并保持任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型（WAMs）在操作基准测试中表现优异，但需要大量去噪步骤，成本过高无法实现实时控制。步骤蒸馏是天然解决方案，但现成方法在联合视频-动作场景中失效，因为视频和动作流使用不同SNR偏移的噪声调度，导致边际噪声分布显著不对称，单模态蒸馏方法无法适应。

Method: 提出Flash-WAM，受一致性蒸馏启发的模态感知步骤蒸馏框架。为每种模态选择一致性函数以匹配其噪声 regime：动作流低噪声区采用线性梯度缩放参数化，视频流高噪声区采用方差保持参数化。该方法基于对一致性函数族的结构分析，刻画了在一致性边界条件下可实现的梯度缩放范围。

Result: 在LingBot-VA上实例化，Flash-WAM将推理压缩到单步。在RoboTwin 2.0上，每块延迟从8.1秒降至348毫秒（23倍加速），实现实时推理。在模拟基准上保持任务成功率（RoboTwin 2.0上85.5%，LIBERO上95.7%），在真实Unitree G1人形机器人上恢复60%的平均性能，而朴素一致性蒸馏在相同步骤预算下仅达到24%。

Conclusion: Flash-WAM通过模态感知的蒸馏策略有效解决了异构噪声调度下的联合视频-动作蒸馏难题，在极大加速推理的同时保持了较好的任务成功率，为世界-动作模型的实时控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) jointly generate future video and robot actions through iterative diffusion, achieving strong performance on manipulation benchmarks but requiring tens of denoising steps, a cost that precludes real-time control. Step distillation has emerged as the natural remedy, but off-the-shelf methods break down in the joint video-action setting because video and action streams use different SNR-shifted noise schedules and reach training with substantially different marginal noise distributions, an asymmetry that single-modality distillation methods cannot accommodate. We introduce \textbf{Flash-WAM}, a modality-aware step-distillation framework inspired by consistency distillation that selects the consistency function for each modality to match its noise regime: a linear-gradient-scaling parametrization for the action stream's low-noise regime, paired with a variance-preserving parametrization for the video stream's high-noise regime, grounded in a structural analysis of the consistency-function family that characterizes the achievable gradient scaling under the consistency boundary condition. Instantiated on LingBot-VA, Flash-WAM compresses inference to a single step in each modality. On RoboTwin 2.0, this reduces per-chunk latency from $8.1$ seconds to $348$ ms on NVIDIA L40S, a $23{\times}$ speedup that enables real-time inference. Flash-WAM preserves task success on simulation benchmarks ($85.5\%$ RoboTwin 2.0, $95.7\%$ LIBERO) and substantially recovers real-world performance ($60\%$ average on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot), while naive consistency distillation drops to $24\%$ at the same step budget.

</details>


### [220] [Scaling Laws for Behavioral Foundation Models over User Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05257)
*Rickard Brüel Gabrielsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了行为基础模型（如推荐系统、支付、欺诈检测等）中的计算最优缩放规律。作者通过约600次实验，发现小嵌入器（约占总参数2%）在所有计算预算下都是最优的，且计算最优训练在低计算量时更偏向数据密集型，但随着计算量增加，其D/N比率向Chinchilla启发式方法靠拢。关键发现是：评估指标本身应作为缩放规律的一部分，因为它会改变计算最优的配置方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然语言模型已建立缩放规律，但行为基础模型（基于用户行为序列训练的模型）仍缺乏类似的计算校准机制，这阻碍了其在大规模部署中的资源优化配置。

Method: 在真实交互数据上进行了约600次运行实验，涵盖$10^{15}$-$10^{19}$ FLOPs的训练计算量，联合变化四个维度：两部分参数分配、关键批次大小、模型/数据分配、冻结嵌入器后的负采样数量。

Result: 小嵌入器（约2%参数）在所有预算下计算最优；计算最优训练在低计算量时数据密集型更强；负采样随预算增加而更偏好更多负样本；评估指标的不同会改变计算最优配方。

Conclusion: 在行为基础模型中，评估指标本身应成为缩放规律的一部分——改变评估指标可能改变计算最优的训练方案。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly trained on sequences of user actions in recommendation, payments, fraud, and commerce, but these models still lack the kind of compute calibration that scaling laws provide for language models. We study a common two-part behavioral-model architecture: a feature-based event embedder maps each multi-modal item to a vector, and a decoder-only transformer predicts the next event from the resulting sequence. Across roughly 600 runs on real interaction data, spanning $10^{15}$-$10^{19}$ training FLOPs, we jointly vary four deployment-relevant axes: the two-part parameter split, critical batch size, model/data allocation, and the number of sampled negatives used after freezing the embedder. A small embedder ($s^{\star}\!\approx\!2\%$ of parameters) is compute-optimal at every budget we test because embedder parameters are both more expensive per step and exposed to far more repeated items than contextualizer parameters. Compute-optimal training is data-heavy relative to text at low compute, but its $D/N$ ratio moves toward the Chinchilla heuristic as compute increases. The sampled training objective and deployed ranking metrics disagree in ways that themselves scale: critical batch size, optimal negative count after freezing, and the agreement between loss and ranking quality all shift with compute and with the chosen evaluation metric. For negative sampling, larger budgets increasingly prefer more negatives; by $10^{19}$ FLOPs the active constraint is candidate-axis memory rather than FLOPs. In behavioral foundation models, the evaluation metric is therefore part of the scaling law: changing it can change the compute-optimal recipe.

</details>


### [221] [Policy-Conditioned Counterfactual Credit for Verifiable Reinforcement Learning of Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05263)
*Renwei Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CVT-RL算法，通过反事实验证奖励、干预有效性门控和策略条件反事实贡献估计器，显著提升长程语言智能体的推理可靠性和任务成功率，并降低作弊行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法中，长程语言智能体仍会学习到无证据支撑的推理链、信念漂移和取巧行为（shortcut actions），而现有过程奖励大多是基于相关性而非因果性，无法评估某一步骤是否真正贡献于最终验证成功。

Method: 提出CVT-RL框架：1) 密集可验证奖励；2) 干预有效性门控（删除、语义替换、证据替换、工具输出扰动四种干预）；3) 策略条件反事实贡献（PCCC）估计器；4) 选择调整的双重稳健估计器增强优势函数；5) 增广拉格朗日方法约束无证据主张、跳过验证、工具篡改和不安全调用。

Result: 在长上下文QA、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld及网页/工具任务上：任务成功率从71.8%（非因果RL）和75.4%（信息匹配反事实基线）提升至78.9%；证据F1从78.9提升至82.8；测量作弊率从7.2%降至3.9%；人工审计作弊率4.6%（基线8.1%）；各主要指标经Holm校正后p<0.01。

Conclusion: 精心设计的反事实信用分配、有效性门控、诊断机制和可验证约束，为长程语言智能体RL提供了一条可复现的、更可靠的路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards improves reasoning and tool use, yet long-horizon language agents still learn unsupported evidence chains, belief drift, and shortcut actions that satisfy terminal checks. Existing process rewards are mostly correlational: they reward retrieval-, reflection-, or verification-like steps without estimating whether the step contributes to final verified success under a specified intervention. We propose CVT-RL, a constrained policy-gradient algorithm with dense verifiable rewards, intervention-validity gating, and a policy-conditioned counterfactual contribution (PCCC) estimator. Deletion, semantic substitution, evidence substitution, and tool-output perturbation define separate controlled interventions; continuations are sampled from a frozen reference policy, and a selection-adjusted doubly robust estimator augments the advantage. Belief control uses only prefix-observable labels, while an augmented Lagrangian constrains unsupported claims, skipped verification, tool tampering, and unsafe calls. On long-context QA, ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and web/tool tasks, CVT-RL improves average task success from 71.8% for compute-matched non-causal RL and 75.4% for an information-matched counterfactual-process baseline to 78.9%, improves evidence F1 from 78.9 to 82.8 over the information-matched baseline, and reduces measured hacking from 7.2% to 3.9%. Independent human audit estimates 4.6% hacking for CVT-RL versus 8.1% for the information-matched baseline, and adaptive detector-evasion attacks raise hacking only to 7.1%. Stratified bootstrap and mixed-effects tests give p<0.01 after Holm correction for all primary metrics. Carefully scoped counterfactual credit, paired with validity gating, diagnostics, and verifiable constraints, provides a reproducible route toward more reliable long-horizon RL for language agents.

</details>


### [222] [REGEN: Reference-Guided Synthetic Multivariate Time Series Generation for Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05264)
*Moulik Gupta,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReGeN是一种参考引导的多元时间序列生成框架，将观测序列分解为周期主干、随机残差和跨变量依赖三个可解释组件，通过可控采样生成覆盖更广分布域、保留领域结构的高质量合成数据，在低数据场景下可替代真实数据甚至超越其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列生成方法存在两大缺陷：基于先验的方法（如TimePFN）产生领域无关的样本，而数据驱动方法（如TimeGAN）将参考数据视为黑箱监督，无法显式控制周期结构、局部变异和跨变量动态，导致生成数据与真实领域结构脱节。

Method: ReGeN将每个参考序列分解为三个可解释组件：1）相位对齐的周期主干（捕获领域主导形态）；2）基于深度核高斯过程的逐变量随机残差；3）通过结构因果模型及拟合耦合系数注入的滞后感知跨变量依赖。通过可控温度对组件进行采样，在拓宽分布覆盖的同时保留领域结构。

Result: 1）ReGeN生成的数据可一致替代真实同源数据，且预测性能损失极小；2）在强周期领域（如交通）中，生成数据甚至优于真实数据；3）在ReGeN语料上预训练的基模型优于基于先验和数据驱动方法的合成数据预训练模型。

Conclusion: 在低数据场景下，如何结构化利用参考数据（即"如何利用"）与数据量本身同样重要。

Abstract: Training robust multivariate time series forecasting models requires large, diverse corpora, yet many real-world domains provide only a handful of observed sequences. Existing generators fail to resolve this mismatch: prior-based approaches (e.g., CauKer, TimePFN) produce domain-agnostic samples, while data-driven methods (e.g., TimeGAN) treat references as black-box supervision, forfeiting explicit control over periodic structure, local variability, and cross-variable dynamics.
  We propose ReGeN, a reference-guided generative pipeline that treats observed sequences not as examples to imitate, but as structural scaffolds for controllable synthesis. ReGeN decomposes each reference into three interpretable components: a phase-aligned periodic backbone capturing dominant domain morphology; per-variable stochastic residuals modeled with a deep-kernel Gaussian process; and lag-aware cross-variable dependencies injected through a structural causal model with fitted coupling coefficients. Sampling these components at controllable temperature broadens distributional coverage while preserving domain-grounded structure.
  We show that ReGeN-generated data consistently substitutes for real sibling data with minimal forecasting degradation, and in strongly periodic domains such as traffic, can outperform the real source itself. We further show that a foundation model pretrained on ReGeN corpora outperforms those pretrained on prior-based and data-driven synthetic alternatives. This suggests that in low-data regimes, how reference data is structurally exploited can matter as much as how much data is available.

</details>


### [223] [Data-efficient flood depth prediction through domain-aware coreset selection and tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05265)
*Lipai Huang,Adithi Srinath,Manas Singh,Junwei Ma,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种领域感知的coreset构建流程，利用表格基础模型进行洪水深度预测，仅需0.7%的训练数据即可达到监督参考模型98.5%的精度（R²=0.663 vs 0.673），并且无需针对特定流域重新训练即可迁移到未见过流域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近实时洪水深度预测需要既准确又快速的代理模型，但现有监督代理模型每个流域需要数百万训练样本且无法外推到原始网格之外，数据效率低且迁移性差。

Method: 提出领域感知的coreset构建流程：按重现期和最受影响流域对风暴进行分层，然后使用目标感知的空间选择器采样六边形区域，在推理时条件化表格基础模型。

Result: 仅用0.7%的每流域训练池，模型在休斯顿地区9个流域平均R²=0.663，达到监督参考（R²=0.673）的98.5%；无需任务特定重训练即可迁移到未见过流域；在远分布外真实风暴案例中超越监督参考。

Conclusion: 领域感知的coreset构建使表格基础模型能够实现数据高效、跨流域可迁移的洪水预测，无需每流域单独训练。

Abstract: Near-real-time flood depth prediction demands surrogate models that are accurate, fast, and transferable across watersheds. Supervised surrogates can match physics-based simulators in accuracy but need millions of training rows per watershed and cannot extrapolate beyond their original mesh. We propose a domain-aware coreset construction pipeline that conditions a tabular foundation model at inference time. The pipeline stratifies storms by return period and most-affected watershed, then samples hexagons with a target-aware spatial selector. With 0.7% of the per-watershed training pool, the model attains a mean $R^2$ of 0.663 across nine Houston-area watersheds, within 98.5% of the supervised reference ($R^2$ = 0.673). It transfers to held-out watersheds without task-specific retraining, staying ahead of a coreset-trained supervised baseline. On real storms it exceeds the supervised reference on a far out-of-distribution case and trails it on a mostly in-distribution one. Domain-aware coreset construction lets tabular foundation models deliver data-efficient, watershed-transferable flood predictions without per-watershed training.

</details>


### [224] [Sharp Low-Degree Thresholds for Planted-vs-Planted Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05266)
*Anda Skeja,Daniel Gutiérrez Espinoza,Fiona Skerman,Alexander S. Wein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了种植-种植设定下低度多项式检验的精确阈值，为 planted submatrix 和 planted dense subgraph 模型中的计数社区问题提供了匹配的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏种植-种植设定下低度多项式检验的精确阈值，且弱检验的阈值行为尚不明确。

Method: 基于低度恢复中的潜变量展开框架，发展新方法识别和修剪非信号贡献。

Result: 获取了与低度恢复阈值精确常数匹配的检验阈值，并揭示了弱检验的光滑过渡现象。

Conclusion: 为种植-种植设定下的低度多项式检验提供了完整阈值理论，并建立了统一的框架。

Abstract: We establish the first sharp thresholds for low-degree polynomial tests in planted-vs-planted settings, where the goal is to determine with vanishing error which of two structured planted mechanisms generated the observed data. We prove matching low-degree upper and lower bounds for counting communities in the planted submatrix and planted dense subgraph models. The resulting testing threshold coincides, down to the sharp constant, with the known low-degree recovery threshold. In contrast, the task of weak testing, where the goal is to outperform random guessing, does not have a sharp threshold but rather a smooth transition, which we identify. To prove our results, we develop a framework for planted-vs-planted testing that builds on a latent-variable expansion originating in low-degree recovery and employs new methods to identify and prune non-signal contributions.

</details>


### [225] [Learning Manifold and Itô Dynamics with Branched Neural Rough Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05272)
*Luke Thompson,Dai Shi,Lequan Lin,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分支神经粗糙微分方程（B-NRDEs），通过Hopf代数框架扩展NRDE，使其能够处理Itô动力学和流形上有序协变导数，并在粗糙Bergomi波动率、SO(3)动力学预测和SPD协方差动力学等任务上展现出统一有效的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准NRDE依赖于洗牌代数（Stratonovich微积分的代数对应），无法表达Itô动力学所需的二次变分项，也无法处理带联络的流形上控制Itô流的有序协变导数，因此需要一种更通用的框架来统一处理欧几里得- Stratonovich之外的随机和流形值动力学。

Method: 提出B-NRDEs，一种Hopf代数框架，将NRDE的log-ODE步骤重新解释为状态空间流形上的几何数值积分，根据控制代数匹配相应的微积分：使用Grossman-Larson根树处理欧几里得Itô动力学，使用Munthe-Kaas-Wright平面根树处理流形上的有序协变导数，使用洗牌代数处理经典Stratonovich情形。同时引入分支签名核目标，使二次变分项在训练中可见，实现Itô一致的法匹配。

Result: 在粗糙Bergomi波动率、sim-to-real SO(3)动力学预测和SPD协方差动力学三个任务上，B-NRDEs在欧几里得-Stratonovich设置之外展现了统一且有效的方法。

Conclusion: B-NRDEs通过代数-几何框架有效扩展了NRDE的适用范围，为随机和流形值动力学提供了一种超越传统欧几里得- Stratonovich框架的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Neural rough differential equations (NRDEs) stay accurate under irregular sampling while taking far fewer integration steps than standard neural differential equations, summarising a finely sampled driver by its log-signature and advancing the hidden state over coarse intervals using the log-ODE method. This efficiency rests on the shuffle algebra, the algebraic counterpart of Stratonovich calculus. This reliance means NRDEs cannot expose the quadratic-variation terms Itô dynamics require, nor the ordered covariant derivatives that govern Itô flows on connection-equipped manifolds. Ameliorating this, we introduce Branched Neural Rough Differential Equations (B-NRDEs), a Hopf-algebraic framework that recasts the NRDE log-ODE step as geometric numerical integration on the state-space manifold, matching the driving algebra to the governing calculus: Grossman--Larson rooted trees for Euclidean Itô dynamics, Munthe-Kaas--Wright planar rooted trees for ordered covariant derivatives on manifolds, and the shuffle algebra in the classical Stratonovich case. This yields intrinsic coarse-step dynamics that exactly preserve manifold constraints. Finally, we introduce a branched signature-kernel objective to enable Itô-consistent law matching by making quadratic-variation terms visible during training. On rough Bergomi volatility, sim-to-real $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ dynamics forecasting, and SPD covariance dynamics, B-NRDEs offer a unified, effective approach to stochastic and manifold-valued dynamics beyond the Euclidean--Stratonovich setting.

</details>


### [226] [Anomaly Detection for Electro-Hydrostatic Actuators using LSTM Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05274)
*Nehal Afifi,Abdelmonem Elhendawi,Felix Leitenberger,Nadine Piat,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对电静液作动器(EHA)传感器数据，提出了一种基于LSTM自编码器的离线异常检测框架，在温度和压力数据上实现了99.0%的平均准确率和极低的误报率，验证了数据驱动方法在EHA异常检测中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHA系统中传感器异常检测对安全可靠运行至关重要，但传统统计和机器学习方法无法捕捉EHA信号的时间依赖性，导致检测精度低、误报率高，且缺乏系统性的数据驱动方法评估。

Method: 采用基于重建的LSTM自编码器，通过验证集重建误差分布进行校准和评估；在受控测试台上收集单变量EHA传感器信号（温度和压力），并在多种故障注入场景下使用准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数进行性能评估，辅以不同工况下的敏感性分析。

Result: LSTM自编码器在全部评估传感器上平均准确率达99.0%，精确率最高100%，召回率在90.2%-99.6%之间，F1分数在93.1%-99.8%之间，展现了高检测灵敏度和极低的误报率。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的离线异常检测方法在EHA系统中是可行的，未来工作将把该框架适配到在线（实时）环境。

Abstract: Electro-Hydrostatic Actuators (EHAs) are widely used in aerospace and industrial systems, where timely detection of sensor anomalies is essential to ensure safe and reliable operation. However, the large volume and high sampling frequency of EHA sensor data pose challenges for accurate and efficient anomaly detection. Conventional statistical and classical machine-learning methods such as Z-score, Interquartile Range (IQR), Median Absolute Deviation (MAD), Isolation Forest, Gaussian Mixture, and k-means often fail to capture the temporal dependencies inherent in EHA signals, resulting in limited detection accuracy and elevated false-alarm rates. Furthermore, systematic evaluations of data-driven anomaly detection approaches for EHA systems remain scarce, particularly under varying operational conditions. This study presents an offline anomaly-detection framework for univariate EHA sensor signals, focusing on temperature and pressure data collected from a controlled test bench. The method employs a reconstruction-based Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) autoencoder, calibrated and evaluated using validation-set reconstruction-error distributions. Performance is assessed across multiple fault-injection scenarios using accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score, complemented by sensitivity analyses under varying operating conditions. The LSTM autoencoder achieved an average accuracy of 99.0\%, precision up to 100\%, recall between 90.2\% and 99.6\%, and F1-scores from 93.1\% to 99.8\%, demonstrating high detection sensitivity and a very low false-alarm rate across all evaluated sensors. These results highlight the feasibility of data-driven offline anomaly detection for EHAs. Future work will focus on adapting the developed framework for an online (real-time) environment.

</details>


### [227] [Agentic Monte Carlo: Simulating Reinforcement Learning for Black-Box Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05296)
*Dae Yon Hwang,Raunaq Suri,Valentin Villecroze,Anthony L. Caterini,Jesse C. Cresswell,Noël Vouitsis,Brendan Leigh Ross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Monte Carlo (AMC)方法，利用强化学习与贝叶斯推断的等价性，通过顺序蒙特卡洛采样直接从最优策略中采样，从而优化黑盒LLM智能体的行为，无需访问模型参数。在AgentGym基准测试中，AMC显著优于提示基线，甚至随着测试时计算量增加而超越GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RL方法无法应用于仅提供API访问的黑盒LLM智能体（如闭源商业模型），因为无法进行参数级别的优化。本文旨在解决这一限制，实现对黑盒智能体的RL风格优化。

Method: 利用RL与贝叶斯推断的等价性，将最优策略定义为轨迹的后验分布，先验为固定的黑盒LLM智能体。采用顺序蒙特卡洛（Sequential Monte Carlo）方法，通过学习价值函数来引导智能体，同时保持底层黑盒模型不变，从后验分布中直接采样最优策略。

Result: 在AgentGym基准的三个不同环境中验证，AMC显著优于提示基线，并且在增加测试时计算量时，甚至超越了GRPO（一种可对开放权重模型进行RL的方法）。

Conclusion: AMC证明了在不对黑盒LLM智能体进行参数级优化的情况下，进行原则性的RL风格优化是可行的，为仅提供API访问的闭源模型提供了一种有效的测试时优化方法。

Abstract: LLM agents operate in two distinct regimes: open-weight agents amenable to reinforcement learning (RL) and black-box agents whose behaviour must be controlled purely at test time.
